36. DISCOVERY CONTRACT CLASSIFICATION
======================================================================
LOCKED OPS-2 DESIGN PRINCIPLES:

1. inventory_items
   -> canonical inventory catalogue candidate

   -> create only if no equivalent canonical entity exists

   -> must not carry authoritative stock balance


2. inventory_lots
   -> lot/batch/expiry/procurement traceability candidate
   -> create only if existing medication/supply lot model cannot be safely reused


3. inventory_transactions
   -> candidate authoritative append-only inventory ledger
   -> receipts/issues/adjustments/returns must be ledger movements
   -> authoritative balance derived from ledger
   -> cached/projected balance permitted only as non-authoritative optimization

4. resident_consumption_events
   -> resident-attributed consumption evidence
   -> NOT a second inventory ledger
   -> inventory-affecting consumption must link to inventory transaction
   -> may link to operational Work Event where care work produced consumption

5. Work Event reuse
   -> operational_work_events remains work evidence
   -> inventory transaction remains stock movement evidence
   -> resident consumption remains resident attribution evidence
   -> one domain must not silently replace another

6. Human authority
   -> canonical staff_actors reused
   -> AI/System cannot autonomously create authoritative stock movement
   -> AI may assist/analyse only under later explicit contract

7. Scalability
   -> no hard resident cap
   -> bounded server-side lists required
   -> indexed item/lot/resident/time queries required
   -> full-history dashboard scans forbidden

8. Scope boundary
   -> OPS-2 inventory + resident consumption only
   -> KPI remains OPS-3
   -> Kitchen remains OPS-4
   -> Cost remains OPS-5
   -> W unified family report integration remains OPS-6
